The new render is the CORRECT one (the old hub was smaller than the tip dots it feeds, which is the bug row 350 fixed), so this is not a regression — but a customer who already approved may open their link and see a different picture with no notice. **POPULATION UNMEASURED — no DB access in S52.** Run this first, then decide whether it needs saying to anyone at all: `select q.quote_number, q.status, d.scene->>'lightScale' as light_scale from designs d join quotes q on q.id = d.quote_id where d.scene ? 'lightScale' and (d.scene->>'lightScale')::numeric <> 1 and exists (select 1 from jsonb_array_elements(d.scene->'items') it where it->>'kind' = 'spritzer') and q.quote_sent_at is not null;` **MEASURED AND CLOSED, S52 post-wrap (the Supabase MCP came online mid-session):** 309 designs total, 13 carry a `lightScale` at all, **5 sit off 1.0x**. Of those five, **four are DRAFTS never sent to anyone**, and two of those four contain no spritzer at all. The entire real-world exposure is **ONE quote — #1293** (real customer, not is_test, sent 2026-08-22, viewed the same evening, **never approved**, lightScale 2.9, 5 spritzers). So the worst case the lens hunted for — an APPROVED design re-rendering differently — has a population of **zero**. One customer who viewed a quote three days ago may see slightly larger spritzer hubs if they open the link again, and the new render is the more correct one. **Decision: nothing to tell anyone; no notice, no re-send.** Recorded rather than shrugged because the reasoning is what makes the next such change cheap to judge. ✅ CLOSED S52. | - | | (S53) |

Jason could not recall whether the page visibly reloaded, so the TRIGGER is unconfirmed - but the exposure does not depend on the trigger. Fix regardless: warn on unload while dirty + show an unsaved-changes indicator; consider edit-mode autosave. Discriminator if wanted: Vercel logs showing two `GET /quote/[id]` for that session. | - |

## Archived at S49 (2026-08-25) - row #393, RESOLVED as SUPERSEDED. The decision the row asked for, made on evidence rather than preference.

The row asked: land the orphaned `jason/288-backfill-script-fix` branch as insurance, or drop it. DROPPED, because the defect it fixes NO LONGER EXISTS ON MASTER, and master's current handling is strictly safer than the branch's.

Evidence, all checked at S49 close:
- The branch was **727 commits behind** master and `git merge-tree` reported conflicts, so landing it was never going to be a clean cherry-pick of a small fix.
- The bug was: a Gmail row with a composite `external_id` and no `source_message_id` fell through to a THREAD-WIDE `modifyThread`, marking every sibling customer's thread read/labeled. The branch's fix was to skip the write-back for that one shape.
- Current master (`src/lib/dashboard/inbox/sync.ts`, the `runHandledWriteback` gmail branch) now reads simply: if `target.sourceMessageId` go message-level, ELSE `sync.gmailLabel = 'skipped'`. There is no thread-wide fallback left at all — `gmailThreadIdFromExternalId` is grep-zero across `src/`, and `modifyThread` has exactly ONE remaining hit, a comment — no call site. (Precision correction from the S49 wrap integration lens: the original wording here said both were grep-zero, which overstated it by one comment. The substance is unchanged — there is no thread-wide call left on the write-back path.). So master skips EVERY row lacking a message id, not merely the composite ones the branch guarded. That is a superset of the orphaned fix.
- Live exposure was separately measured at zero before the decision: 553 gmail items, 22 composite, 20 exposed, and **0 of the 20 still open** (all closed 2026-08-18 by the backfill run itself).

The three commits were archived as patches OUTSIDE the repo before deletion (session scratchpad, `branch-archive/0001..0003`) rather than trusted to memory, then the worktree `.claude/worktrees/agent-a70c8c5d532aa1159` was removed and the branch deleted. Nothing of value was discarded unrecorded.

| # | Task | Size | Notes | Old # |
|---|------|------|-------|-------|
| 393 | ⛔ RESOLVED-SUPERSEDED S49 (dropped, not landed — master already fixes it more thoroughly) — **An unmerged branch holds a real #288 Gmail write-back fix that never got a PR - land it or drop it deliberately (S49 recon)** | XS | `jason/288-backfill-script-fix` (3 commits, worktree `.claude/worktrees/agent-a70c8c5d532aa1159`, no PR ever opened) carries a staff-HIGH fix: a backfilled Gmail row has a composite `external_id` with no `source_message_id`, so `runHandledWriteback` falls through to `modifyThread`, marking EVERY sibling customer's thread read/labeled. MEASURED in S49 rather than assumed: 553 gmail items, 22 composite, **20 exposed, and 0 of the 20 still open** - every one was already closed on 2026-08-18 by the backfill run itself. So the fix is DEFENSIVE ONLY today (it guards a re-run of `scripts/backfill-gml-threads.ts`), not a live bug, which is why it did not jump the queue. Decide: land it as insurance before any future backfill, or close the branch and delete the worktree. | - |

| 366 | ✅ SHIPPED S48 (PR #920, merged+live 2026-08-24, master da31c034) — **Follow-up Done can RESURRECT a completed/dismissed inbox item back to 'handled' - the positive gate is not carried through to the write (S47 wrap technical lens MED, from #901)** | S | src/app/api/dashboard/followup/route.ts (~43-51) reads the anchored item's status via getItemStatus, gates POSITIVELY on it being 'unresponded' via shouldResolveAnchoredItem, then writes through markItemHandledLocal - whose own CAS is a NEGATIVE `.neq('status','handled')` (src/lib/dashboard/inbox/store.ts:1119). That filter refuses an already-handled row but NOT one that moved to 'completed' or 'dismissed' in the gap between the read and the write, so a concurrent /api/dashboard/completed or /dismiss on the same item can be silently resurrected. This is the repo's own positive-seam-gate pitfall: the positive check is real but lives in a pure predicate over a value read EARLIER, and nothing carries it to the write. Note the author already documented the exact hazard (store.ts:2585-2592: 'that guard alone would let a completed/dismissed item get resurrected to handled, which this function exists to prevent') - the comment claims a protection the code shape does not deliver across the TOCTOU gap. NOT a one-liner: markItemHandledLocal has three callers (followup, handled, reply) where `.neq` is correct, so the fix is an expected-status parameter or a variant, not a global CAS change. Narrow blast radius (needs two concurrent operator actions on one item); no test exercises the race. | - |
| 364 | ✅ SHIPPED S48 (PR #920, merged+live 2026-08-24, master da31c034) — **ScheduleDay hides its own staleness warning on a FAILED refetch, so a dispatcher can read the previous day's crew data under a new date (S47 wrap staff lens MED - a regression introduced by S47's own #897 fix round)** | S | src/components/ops/ScheduleDay.tsx (~44-93, 136-190). #897's first cut blanked the whole view with a skeleton on every date change; the fix round kept the previous day visible with a 'Loading {date}... (showing {loadedDate} below)' label. But that label is gated on `loading`, not on `loadedDate !== date` - so the moment a failed refetch flips `loading` false the warning DISAPPEARS while the stale content stays, leaving a new date in the picker over the previous day's crew/capacity numbers with only a generic red error line as the tell. Fix: gate the staleness label on `loadedDate !== date` so it survives the error path, which is exactly the path that needs it. | - |
| 365 | ✅ SHIPPED S48 (PR #920, merged+live 2026-08-24, master da31c034) — **Follow-up Done resolves the anchored inbox item server-side but does not refresh the list, so it can linger as open for up to 25s (S47 wrap staff lens LOW, from #901)** | XS | src/components/dashboard/inbox/FollowUpStrip.tsx + src/app/api/dashboard/followup/route.ts (~41-53). The server-side coupling works; the client just never calls router.refresh(), so the item stays visible in the main inbox list until the next 25s poll. Jason did not build this and asked for it explained) --- FOUR lists from TWO tables.** (1) Main inbox 'Open leads' = `inbox_items` `status='unresponded' AND followed_up_at IS NULL` (`store.ts listOpenItems`). (2) 'Awaiting their reply' = `followed_up_at IS NOT NULL AND status NOT IN (completed,dismissed)` (`listInWorks`) - **the key is `followed_up_at`, NOT status**. (3) 'Handled' = `status='handled' AND followed_up_at IS NULL`. (4) 'Follow-ups due today' = **the separate `follow_ups` table**, pending + due today (`listDueFollowUps`), which **never reads the inbox item's status** - so it overlaps freely with 1/2/3 while 1/2/3 are mutually exclusive. **Why Handled vs Awaiting-reply feels arbitrary:** replying IN-TOOL stamps BOTH `status='handled'` AND `followed_up_at` (`api/dashboard/reply/route.ts`), so a replied conversation lands in 'Awaiting their reply'. **'Handled' therefore actually means 'marked done WITHOUT replying'.** **Yellow label:** exactly one text exists app-wide - `Follow up - {N}d quiet` (`InWorksSection.tsx` + `isStale()` in `lifecycle.ts`), purely an AGE test (`now - lastActivityAt > followUpDays`, default 3, DB-backed `app_settings` key `dashboard.followUpDays`, editable at /inbox/settings). It is computed AFTER bucketing and never feeds back into it. **Jason's stated wish ('no yellow label => not in Handled') is INCOHERENT as written** - everything enters Handled with NO label; the label only appears once stale. It would empty Handled of everything recent. Real options to choose between: (a) Handled = done, disappears, only returns on a new inbound; (b) Handled = holding pen, label is the nag (today); (c) MERGE Handled into Awaiting-reply into one 'In the works' list sorted by quiet-time. NOTE a second, unrelated 3-day threshold exists (`DASHBOARD_CONFIG.sentNoReplyStaleDays`, homepage Worklist) - two independent knobs, same default, easy to confuse. **--- WHY CALLS DO NOT REGISTER --- (a) PROVEN, `store.ts:201`:** `skip: !existing && touch.direction === 'outbound' && !TRACKS_OUTBOUND_FIRST_OBSERVATION.has(touch.source)` where the allowlist is ONLY `['quotetool']`. So any outbound GHL touch on a conversation with no existing item is **silently discarded - no row, no contact, no activity log**. Deliberate anti-noise rule written for cold outreach texts; it is channel-blind so it swallows calls. **(b) COUNTER-INTUITIVE:** for an item that ALREADY exists, an outbound call ALREADY auto-marks it handled - `isAnsweredByDirection` is DEFINED in `escalation.ts` ~79 and merely CONSUMED by `reducer.ts` ~51, and its tests live in `escalation.test.ts` ~88-95, NOT `store.test.ts` (accuracy-lens correction 2026-08-11 - both original attributions were wrong). So 'make outbound count' is already true for existing items. **(c) THE BLOCKER - do a PROBE before building:** **no code in this repo has ever observed a real GHL call event.** The `TYPE_CALL`/`TYPE_VOICEMAIL` -> `channel:'call'` map in `ghl.ts` was written from documentation/assumption - no fixture, no logged payload, and the original spike never exercised a call. **Unknown: does GHL's dialer even update `/conversations/search`'s `lastMessageType`/`lastMessageDate` the way SMS/email do?** If not, every line of that mapping is dead. **First slice = place one real call + one real voicemail through GHL and LOG what arrives** (one question per test, per the S29 trap-first-probe lesson) - an hour that de-risks the whole channel. **Call OUTCOME (missed/cancelled/voicemail-left) is architecturally invisible:** ingest reads only the coarse conversation summary; `getConversationMessages()` (which could carry per-message detail) is wired but its ONLY caller is the AI-draft route. May need a new PIT - **GHL PIT scopes are IMMUTABLE, adding one means issuing a new token**. **`event_type` is a DEAD COLUMN** - exists in schema with a comment, populated by NO adapter, **all 799 prod rows null** - the natural home for call/voicemail/outcome typing. **--- THE DUPLICATE PROBLEM IS MOSTLY NOT DUPLICATES --- Prod snapshot 2026-08-11 16:xx UTC (these counts drift daily - re-run before relying on them; a same-day re-check already read 803/346): 799 rows / 345 contacts / 0 null contact_id.** status: dismissed 348, unresponded 285, completed 108, handled 58. source: gmail 445, quotetool 204, ghl 150. channel: email 456, app 202, sms 86, call 42, null 11. **NOT an idempotency bug: ZERO rows share `(source,external_id)` and ZERO share `source_message_id`.** The code's `onConflict:'source,external_id'` matches the enforced unique index exactly. **(1) 39% of the table is the company emailing ITSELF** - contact `18d5dd86` ('Yule Love Lights', sales@mail.yulelovelights.com) holds **313 of 799 rows**, all Gmail: marketing sends, 'Quote viewed' alerts, the escalation email, the EOD digest - our own automated mail polled back in as customer leads. **(2) Marketing newsletters ingested as leads** - 'Best of Long Island' (bestof@schnepsmedia.com) 20 rows, another sender 12; each send is a new Gmail thread id so it accumulates, and 5 of them sit in the LIVE awaiting-reply list right now. **(3) Genuine customer duplication is BY DESIGN and much smaller than it looks** - excluding the internal contact: 486 rows / 344 contacts, of which **274 have exactly ONE row**. Only 70 have >1, and 41 of those are legitimately multi-source (a customer quoted + texted + emailed = 3 rows, one per source system). Row identity is `(source, external_id)` = one row per conversation PER SOURCE, deliberately not per customer (the migration comment says so). `InboxList.tsx` renders rows flat with no `contact_id` grouping. **So 'one row per customer' = a DISPLAY-LAYER rollup + ingest hygiene, NOT a dedupe/key fix** - unless the row model itself changes, which is a far bigger move. **--- BONUS BUG FOUND (real customer, stuck 13 days) --- the ONE stuck `:color-request` item** (`6cd9314e-df98-4d51-9e0f-1cb4c6ce75ea`, quote #1129, christine1022@aol.com, $761.25) is invisible AND can never escalate because its quote carries `legacy_rebook = true`, and `excludeLegacyRebookItems` (#157/#181) filters legacy-rebook quotetool items out of **both** `listOpenItems` AND `listEscalatableItems` - `dashboard_activity` shows 3 `ingested` rows on 2026-07-29 and never an `escalated` one. The blanket Neighbor-hiding rule defeats the color-request flow's own documented intent (per `closeQuoteInboxNoise`'s comment) of staying live after the parent quote resolves. **A live customer colour-change request nobody can see.** Check how many other legacy_rebook quotes carry the same. **--- SLICES (pick up in roughly this order) --- A.** PROBE: real call + voicemail through GHL, log the payload, settle whether calls are visible at all (do FIRST, blocks the rest of the call work). **B.** Ingest hygiene: stop polling our own sales@->sales@ mail and known newsletter senders into the inbox - biggest single signal win (39% of rows), and cheap. **C.** Decide the bucket semantics (a/b/c above) WITH Naldo, then make `lifecycle.ts bucketOf()` the single source of truth - it already models all 5 buckets correctly and is **dead code**, with the real logic hand-rolled twice as raw Supabase filters that nothing keeps in sync. **D.** Display-layer rollup: one row per customer, expandable to its per-source conversations. **E.** Couple the follow-up strip to the item: clicking Done on a follow-up currently does NOT touch the inbox item, so the strip clears while the conversation stays open elsewhere. **F.** Cold-outbound exception so an outbound CALL on a never-ingested conversation creates its 'we reached out' record (depends on A). **G.** Fix the legacy-rebook colour-request invisibility above, and clear the stuck row. **H.** Naming: 'Awaiting their reply' (customer owes us) vs the main list (we owe them) are both 'awaiting reply' - genuinely ambiguous, rename as part of C. **--- JASON'S RULINGS 2026-08-11 (locked, do not re-ask) --- (1) HANDLED MEANS DONE:** option (a) - a handled item leaves the screen entirely; it only returns if the customer sends a NEW inbound. Not a holding pen, no yellow-label nag on it. This supersedes the 'no yellow label => not in Handled' phrasing (which was incoherent, see above) - the yellow-label concept survives only for 'Awaiting their reply', where waiting on the customer is the point. **(2) NEIGHBOR EXCLUSION NARROWS:** replace the blanket `excludeLegacyRebookItems` hide-everything rule with **hide ONLY neighbor quotes that are DRAFTED AND UNSENT**. Any neighbor quote that is sent / viewed / approved / booked behaves like a normal quote in the inbox and in escalation. This is what unblocks the christine1022@aol.com colour-request above (quote #1129 is BOOKED and was still hidden) and it keeps #157/#181's original intent (don't nag about drafts nobody sent) without swallowing live customer asks. Applies to `listOpenItems` AND `listEscalatableItems` - **AND TO A THIRD SITE: Naldo's concurrent PR #734 (`naldo/223-named-morning-brief`, open 2026-08-11) mints a NEW `isLegacyRebook` computation inside `listDueFollowUps` that REPLICATES (does not call) the same blanket pattern.** Grep for all three before building; fixing only the two named here leaves the third silently hiding neighbor quotes - exactly how the christine1022 request went invisible. This is the repo's sibling-guard-parity pitfall about to recur a third time in one file. **--- SLICE B ROOT-CAUSED (2026-08-11) - the self-ingest filter EXISTS and has a HOLE, do not rebuild it --- ** `gmail.ts` already carries a `GmailIdentity` ({ourEmail, ourDomain, internalAddrs}) built in `sync.ts` ~215-220 from `GMAIL_USER` + `HIGHLEVEL_EMAIL_FROM`, and commit `9d04d564` ('treat our whole domain as from-us (escalation self-ingest)') was an earlier attempt at exactly this. **It is still ingesting: 22 self-notification rows this week, newest 2026-08-11 16:12:44 UTC.** **The hole is a SIBLING-PARITY gap in the domain matcher.** `classify.ts isFromUs` does `addr.endsWith('@' + ourDomain)` - **exact domain only**. `quotetool.ts internalQuoteRecipientSuppression` (fixed under #220) does `domain === internalDomain || domain.endsWith('.' + internalDomain)` - **bare domain + any subdomain**. Our own mail is sent from `sales@mail.yulelovelights.com`; against an `ourDomain` of `yulelovelights.com` the exact matcher FAILS and the subdomain-aware one would pass. **Fix = reuse the quotetool predicate in `classify.ts`** (and consider hoisting it to one shared helper so the two cannot drift a third time). **Verify first:** `GMAIL_USER` is a masked Vercel env the assistant cannot read - confirm its actual value before declaring the subdomain mismatch the whole story; the matcher asymmetry is proven either way. **Separately, the newsletter half is NOT the same bug:** a suppression list already exists (`suppression.ts`, `app_settings` key `dashboard.suppressedSenders`, fed by the 'Not a lead' dismiss) and both `ghl.ts` and `gmail.ts` consult it - so check whether dismissing a newsletter sender actually suppresses future sends before building anything new; 'Best of Long Island' has 15 dismissed rows AND 5 still unresponded, which suggests it does not (each send is a new thread id, and suppression matches on SENDER, so it should have caught them - worth a direct test). `classifyMessage` also already returns 'automated' on a `List-Unsubscribe` header - check why that is not catching newsletters. **--- SLICE A: PROBE DONE 2026-08-11, AND IT OVERTURNED THIS ROW'S BIGGEST ASSUMPTION - READ THIS BEFORE THE PROTOCOL BELOW --- ** Jason ran four real calls on the `Naldoven Seizeme` contact and the results were read back off the live GHL API. **CALLS ARE NOT INVISIBLE. `TYPE_CALL` is real and common - 11 of the 40 newest conversations.** The `ghl.ts` mapping is therefore NOT dead code, and everything below that speculates otherwise is superseded. What was actually recorded, per case: **(i) outbound call, answered** -> `TYPE_CALL` / `outbound` / status **`completed`**. **(ii) inbound call, answered** -> **`TYPE_IVR_CALL`** / `inbound` / `completed`. **(iii) inbound call + voicemail left** -> **`TYPE_IVR_CALL`** / `inbound` / `completed`, PLUS a separate notification EMAIL ('Naldoven Seizeme just left a voicemail'). **(iv) inbound call, hung up before connecting** -> **NO message row at all** - 'call cancelled' may simply not be detectable through this API. Earlier calls on the same contact also show `TYPE_CALL` / `outbound` / **`no-answer`**. **🔴 THE HEADLINE BUG: inbound calls arrive as `TYPE_IVR_CALL`, which is NOT in `CHANNEL_BY_TYPE`** (`ghl.ts` maps only TYPE_SMS/EMAIL/CALL/VOICEMAIL/FACEBOOK/INSTAGRAM), so every inbound call falls through to `channel: null`. That is the concrete 'someone called us and the inbox didn't notice' defect - a small, well-scoped fix. **`TYPE_VOICEMAIL` never appeared at all** - a voicemail is a completed inbound IVR call plus a notification email, so voicemail detection must NOT key on `TYPE_VOICEMAIL`; that mapping is aspirational. **Call OUTCOME is available after all** - the message-level `status` field carries `completed`/`no-answer`. Ingest reads only the coarse conversation summary, which is why it looked architecturally invisible. `getConversationMessages()` is already written and wired (its only caller today is the AI-draft route). **No new PIT scope needed.** Also surfaced by the same sweep and NOT in `CHANNEL_BY_TYPE`: `TYPE_CAMPAIGN_EMAIL`, `TYPE_NO_SHOW`, `TYPE_ACTIVITY_CONTACT`, `TYPE_ACTIVITY_OPPORTUNITY` - the `ACTIVITY_*` pair ('Opportunity created', 'DnD enabled by user') is pure system noise that should never become a lead. **Still untested: outbound call -> voicemail (Jason leaving one).** Not blocking. The original protocol is kept below for reference and for that one remaining case: **--- SLICE A PROBE PROTOCOL (original, mostly now ANSWERED above) --- ** the question is whether GHL's dialer surfaces calls in `/conversations/search` at all, and with what `lastMessageType`. Existing tool: `scripts/spikes/ghl-conversations.ts` (READ-ONLY, `npx tsx scripts/spikes/ghl-conversations.ts`, reads creds from `.env.local`, never mutates, prints the mark-read PUT without sending it). Use a NON-CUSTOMER number (Jason's own mobile or a test contact) so no real customer record is touched - see the S47 lesson about test identifiers colliding with live rows. Cover these cases so the mapping can be written from data instead of assumption: (i) OUTBOUND call answered, (ii) OUTBOUND call rung out to voicemail WITH a message left, (iii) INBOUND call answered, (iv) INBOUND call missed / to voicemail, (v) a cancelled/hung-up-before-connect call if GHL can produce one. Note the wall-clock time of each so the returned `lastMessageDate` can be matched to the right event. Then run the spike and record, per case: `lastMessageType`, `lastMessageDirection`, `lastMessageDate`, `lastMessageBody`, and whether a NEW conversation was created or an existing one updated. **If calls never appear in `/conversations/search`, the whole `TYPE_CALL` mapping in `ghl.ts` is dead code and the channel needs a different source entirely** (message-level `getConversationMessages`, a GHL webhook, or a new PIT scope - PIT scopes are IMMUTABLE, adding one means a new token). **More slices will be appended as Jason learns the system** - he flagged that up front. | - |

A quote created and sent between two 5-minute reconcile ticks is only ever observed as outbound → no item → no follow-up. Live proof: quote #1263 (a real customer) created 16:32:54.95, sent 16:33:02.70 — a 7.75s draft window — has neither row, while #1265 (4m21s), #1264 (46m) and #1262 (4h16m) have both. The new item auto-resolves to `handled`/escalation 0 (test-verified), so it anchors the follow-up without entering the open inbox list. ACCEPTED TRADEOFF, recorded in the code: sent `legacy_rebook` quotes are included, which is 2 rows today but would mint one follow-up per quote if the ~114–124 Neighbor pool is ever sent as a scripted wave — stagger the sends or cap per-run creation if that is built. | — |

Gate follow-up creation on real-customer quotes — POSITIVE-match (`is_test` + a known staff/internal contact list, per the positive-seam-gate pitfall), and LOG every suppressed creation (a silent miss on a real customer is worse than today's noise — S53 lens finding). Acceptance criterion: explain why Yelena's same-day sent quote produced NO pending follow-up while Sharon's/julia's did. **✅ SHIPPED S57 2026-08-07 — PR #722 MERGED (master c2d3beaa).** Positive-match on a company-owned email domain (bare + subdomain, case-insensitive); logged and counted via `followUpsSuppressed`. An amend that re-syncs the invoice UPWARD while the Valor call is in flight → the charge captures the OLD (lower) balance, every #170 guard passes (amount matches the stale read, status still settle-able), and the invoice settles paid against the NEW higher total = silent under-charge. | 226 | **🔴 NCE-to-false must RESET `inputs.depositPercent` - two live paths don't (S35 wrap, customer lens; TWO HIGHs, one root cause) - MONEY, live on prod now** - nothing treats "NCE resolves to false" as a positive signal to reset the deposit; only `true` is handled. (a) **Admin NceToggle OFF** (`nce/route.ts` ~117) DELETES the `depositPercent` key (`const {depositPercent:_drop, ...rest}`) instead of writing an explicit blank. `chargesFromResult(result, undefined)` then falls through to the STALE `result.depositRate` (0.4, frozen by whatever Calculate ran while NCE was on) - so the live portal, the approval snapshot's `snapshotDepositRate` AND `currentDepositUsd`, and the actual Valor charge all stay at 40% on a now-untagged quote. `NceToggle`'s own confirm copy promises "reverts to blank (50%)". The BUILDER chip is CORRECT by contrast: `resolveNceDepositPercent` returns `0`, an explicit blank that `effectiveDepositRate` maps to the 50% default. **Fix: write `depositPercent: 0`, do not delete the key.** (b) **`rebookLastSeason`** resolves `is_nce` from the CUSTOMER's current tag (deliberate - they may have been re-tagged since), but `applyNceDepositDefault` only ADDS 40 when true; when false the source quote's 40 passes through untouched (`stripDiscountAndReferralCredit` strips discount/referralCredit, never depositPercent). A customer who LEFT the barter network gets rebooked `is_nce=false` but priced and charged at 40%. `rebookFromQuote` is NOT affected (copies both fields together, so they cannot disagree). Direction of harm on both: UNDER-collection (~10 points of deposit), never an overcharge - no customer is financially harmed, YLL collects less. No test covers "absent depositPercent + a stale non-default `result.depositRate`". | S | ✅ SHIPPED + LIVE (PR #736, master `344f196a`, 2026-08-11) - the live money bug is CLOSED | - |

It renders nothing (no `points`), so there is no way to select or delete it in the editor - but `projectScene`'s `forCategory('mini')` still emits it, so every Calculate re-projects it into `inputs.miniLightItems` and it keeps billing its `stringCount`. **Deleting the entry from `inputs` is futile** - the design regenerates it on the next Calculate (proven the hard way: two SQL deletes were both undone by a re-price). REAL INSTANCE, fixed by hand this session: quote `d91b7e28-d85e-4c54-b97d-1fa024886b97` (#1264, julia lee, sent/unapproved) carried orphan `miniGroup` id `899d6083fa40`, surface `tree`, `stringCount` 8, 14 dead `memberIds`, billing $360 on a $6,438 quote; removed from BOTH `designs.scene.items` (design `c33d0d23-2690-4e5e-aa67-6e9e99d7a842`, 100 -> 99 items) and `quotes.inputs.miniLightItems`, strands untouched. FIX (pick at build): the editor should delete a `miniGroup` when its last member strand goes (root cause), AND/OR `projectScene` should defensively skip a `miniGroup` whose members resolve to nothing (stops the billing even where an orphan already exists). **Then sweep**: read-only, count `miniGroup` items with zero matching `strand` groupIds across all live quotes - an orphan on an APPROVED or PAID quote is materially worse to correct than this one was (the frozen approval snapshot is already signed). | 186 | **Photo upload 413 — client-side downscale before upload (Jason S32, live incident).** Adding a 2nd photo to a quote failed "Couldn't add the photo: 413": every photo upload path (DesignEditor addPhoto, QuoteBuilder main photo ~:1494, satellite ~:2000) base64-JSONs the RAW file → +33% inflation → Vercel's hard 4.5MB request cap 413s anything over ~3.4MB — i.e. most modern phone photos; photo 1 only ever worked when the file happened to be small. Fix: shared client util — canvas downscale (longest edge ~2560px, JPEG q≈0.85; the AI analyzer downscales server-side to less than that anyway per W5-007), wired into all three paths; decode-failure (e.g. HEIC in Chrome) falls back to the raw path (current behavior). Kills 413 for real phone photos + ~10× faster uploads + smaller storage. **▶ S32 phase 1 SHIPPED (PR #653)** — all base64-JSON paths wired. **▶ phase 2 BUILDING (S32):** the deferred MULTIPART paths bit live next day ("Unexpected token 'R', Request En..." = Vercel's plain-text 413 parsed as JSON): wire downscale into the holiday analyze upload (the app's busiest photo path) + training auto-analyze; size-precheck (not flatten) the transparent-PNG custom-graphics upload; graceful non-JSON error handling at every upload site (413 → "photo too large", never a parse error). **▶ phase 2 SHIPPED (PR #656, deploy 80cbc254)** — holiday analyze wired via downscaleForUploadAsBlob (canvas.toBlob core, dataUrl variant proven behavior-identical); training auto-analyze verified already-safe (re-posts phase-1-downscaled state); custom-graphics kept PNG-intact w/ 4MB precheck; shared readUploadErrorMessage at all 5 sites. | S | ✅ **S32** (both phases live) | — |

Breaks are UNPAID per contract §8, so paid time = clock envelope − break time; that arithmetic was built TEST-FIRST because it feeds payout math. Guards, each tested: overlapping breaks merged into their union (naive summing UNDERPAYS), breaks clipped to the envelope, integer milliseconds throughout, never negative, NaN skipped, DB check constraint against negative break time (which would OVERPAY), and idempotent writes (a double-tap returns the running break rather than opening a second or restarting its clock). (2) The stage-mousedown deselect-and-return (`if(selectedIds.size||selectedYardstickId){clearSelection();return}`) SWALLOWS the first on-item drag-draw gesture whenever anything is selected → guard it for the on-item drag-draw case. (3) The strand mouseup calls `redrawScene()` UNCONDITIONALLY → on a no-drag click it `destroyChildren()`s the node before Konva fires the click → click-to-select silently breaks → only redraw when a strand actually committed (hypot≥6). (4) The tool-toggle sidebar handlers (`#categories`/`#bulb-types`/`#decor-types`/`#garland-styles`/`#styles`) call `renderSidebar()` ONLY (never `redrawScene()`) → render-time draggable flags go stale → a drag both MOVES and DRAWS → those handlers must refresh the canvas (or use a `dragstart`→`stopDrag()` guard reading live state). **Note:** the bistro-pole precedent works only because it NEVER preserves click-to-select on poles — Option A's "select on click-without-drag" is the hard part that breaks here. — ORIGINAL SPEC BELOW — **Editor: draw a strand starting ON TOP of an existing strand (Jason S11)** — ⚠️ **SHARED EDITOR CORE change → relay to the design-tool AI** (must land in both to stay byte-identical). Want: in DRAW mode a gesture starting over a strand should draw THROUGH it, not select. **Precedent:** the bistro-pole fall-through already does this for poles (`editor.ts:4321-4323`). **Decision — OPTION A, CHOSEN (Jason S11):** defer the select-vs-draw decision — mousedown over a strand starts a draw; if the pointer **drags**, draw THROUGH the strand below; if it's a **click without drag**, select the strand (preserves click-to-select; more work than B). Defense-in-depth hardening (RLS, audit trail, PII retention, error boundaries) spun off → **#90**. _(Original #80-audit context below.)_ The 2 CRITICALs + the HIGH/MEDIUM auth cluster: there is **no server-side auth** on operator pages (`/customers`, `/admin/*`, `/settings`, `/quote/*`, `/training`, `/insights`, `/inventory`) — they gate data *calls* via a static sessionStorage `x-admin-secret`, not *renders*, so SSR leaks customer PII to anonymous visitors; and most write/CRM APIs (`highlevel/contacts`, settings, training, corrections, uploads, designs, quote create/reprice) have no gate at all. **Fix (one perimeter, not per-page):** a real operator session (httpOnly signed cookie) enforced in a NEW `src/middleware.ts` matcher covering all operator routes + a shared `adminAuth` gate reused by every write/CRM API (replace the static shared secret); also enable RLS on every table, add a creator/actor audit trail, define a PII-retention/image-lifecycle policy, and add customer-facing error/not-found boundaries. Exact file:line per finding in `docs/audit/AUDIT-2026-06-26.md`. **⚖️ Council notes (S14 — ran the `llm-council` skill on sequencing; verdict captured here):** **contain FIRST, then build the real perimeter deliberately — do NOT do the full multi-day rebuild as the first action while exposure is live.** (1) **Stop the bleeding fast (minutes–hours):** ranked options — (a) server-side guard on the 2 CRITICAL routes (`/customers` SSR + `GET /api/integrations/highlevel/contacts`) so anonymous = 403; (b) a broader infra kill-switch over the whole operator surface (Vercel Deployment Protection / password / IP allowlist); (c) purge + reseed prod PII with synthetic data (kills exposure on ALL surfaces, not just known routes). | 73 | ✅ **DONE S13 (PR `jason/multi-select-drag-fix-72`, commit `5d9a85f`, pushed — Jason verified multi-drag on device).** ⓘ *Was tentatively #72; renumbered #72→#73 (2026-06-25) because Naldo's "Jobber historical-data import" already holds #72 on master — this fix's branch/PR/commit names still say "#72" (historical).* BUG: marquee-select several items + drag → only one moved, the rest snapped back. **Root cause:** Konva's Transformer `_proxyDrag` already drags all selected nodes together (each fires its own `dragend` → its own `bakeTransformInto*`), but each bake ended with a **synchronous `redrawScene()`** (destroy+recreate all nodes); the first sibling's dragend redrew and wiped the not-yet-baked siblings → they reverted. **Fix:** a `finishBake()` helper that calls `scheduleSave()` immediately then **defers + coalesces `commit()` + `redrawScene()` into ONE microtask per gesture** (guarded by `bakeFinalizeScheduled` + the `destroyed` flag), wired into all 10 transform/drag bakes. Every sibling folds its new position into the scene before the single re-render; also makes a multi-move ONE undo step + fixes multi-RESIZE. `deleteSelected`/`pasteFromClipboard` left synchronous (commented). **✅ RELAY DONE — design tool mirrored VERBATIM, byte-identical at `b1ee4a9`** (`client/src/pages/editor.ts`). Adversarial review (16 agents) → no real bugs. Gates: tsc · lint 0/2 · 272 tests. | M | ✅ S13; relay done | — |

| 190 | **Street-photo re-analyze nulled the live satellite scale — hallucinated satellite lines counted as "satellite evidence" (prod-diagnosed S33, quote ef73b2de/thomas volberg).** `applyAnalysisResult`'s #97 guard gated satellite-state application on `satelliteSantasLines`/`satelliteGingerbreadLines` being non-empty — but `/api/analyze-photo` (street-only: `reanalyzeCurrent` + the manual-upload handler) never shows the model a satellite image, and its output schema still asks for those two arrays, so the model can hallucinate coordinates for a satellite it never saw. A street re-analyze with hallucinated lines then ran `setSatelliteFeetPerPixel(data.satelliteFeetPerPixel ?? null)` → nulled the live scale from an earlier analyze-address, and clobbered the good seeded lines — the footage-derive effect early-returns forever on a null scale, so every satellite draw (stake/C9/santas/gingerbread) silently measured 0 while the form kept the street-AI footage estimates. Quote ef73b2de was sent with hand-measured stake footage as a same-day workaround. **Fixed (S33):** (1) client gate now requires an ACTUAL satellite payload (`satelliteBase64`/`satelliteFeetPerPixel`, never line content) — `hasSatellitePayload` extracted to `src/lib/design/analysisSatellitePayload.ts` (unit-tested); the scale is only ever overwritten when the payload's fpp is non-null (never null-clobbers); (2) `/api/analyze-photo` strips `satelliteSantasLines`/`satelliteGingerbreadLines` server-side as a belt-and-braces — a street-only result can never carry satellite lines regardless of the client gate; (3) the drawn-line default-label ternary (QuoteBuilder.tsx + `/training/new`) fell through to "new c9 run" for stake runs — added the missing `'new stake run'` branch. | S | ✅ S33 | — |

| 267 | **[SHIPPED+LIVE S38 (PR #762, master cc55bdc8)] `normalizeQuoteTouch`'s `answered` still reads timestamps only for a NON-dead quote — two structural holes, 0 prod rows today (S38 wrap of #266, technical lens LOW ×2)** | S | Both are the SAME class as #266 (a state that means "answered" that the touch never consults) but a different state, so they were deliberately kept OUT of #266's diff rather than widening its scope. **(a)** `answered = isDeadQuote(q) \|\| !!(q.customer_approved_at \|\| q.quote_sent_at)` never consults `deposit_paid_at`. Verified 0 such rows in prod (every `legacy_rebook` row with `deposit_paid_at` set also has `customer_approved_at` set), so theoretical today; `deriveStatus` already returns `'booked'` for that shape, so the fix is one more OR term plus a test. **(b)** The `legacy_rebook` suppression at `quotetool.ts` ~58 gates on the RAW `q.status === 'draft'` (deliberate parity with `store.ts`'s `isHiddenLegacyRebookQuote`, per #252 slice G) and runs BEFORE `answered` is computed — so a legacy_rebook row that was paid offline while its `status` column never advanced past `'draft'` is suppressed to `null` entirely: no inbox item, no follow-up, vanishes. Also 0 rows today (all `legacy_rebook` booked rows have `status` advanced). Related: **#263** already tracks the three-definitions drift on that same guard. Separately, at least **6 other sites** re-implement the declined/cancelled/abandoned literal set on different shapes (`convert-to-job/route.ts` ~137 + ~201, `simulate-deposit/route.ts` ~117 + ~143, `approve/route.ts` ~688, the Valor webhook ~682) — sibling-parity class, not mechanically unifiable since they take a `lifecycle` string or raw SQL rather than a `DashboardQuote`; Jason's call whether that earns its own row. | - |

Today tagging is entirely POST-HOC: draw, select, then pick from the `#sel-surface` dropdown in the selection sidebar (`editor.ts` ~2618-2626, options computed per `sharedBulbType` at ~2577-2581). **The seam is clean and already exists:** `ToolState` (`editor.ts` ~108-155, instantiated as `tool` ~309) already holds every pre-configure-before-drawing default (bulbType, spacing, drawingStyle, colorPattern, bistro sag) and new items inherit them via `quoteDefaultsForNewStrand()` (~4514-4518, called from `commitStrand`/`commitTraceSegments`/`commitSingle`) - whose own comment already says "operator still sets `surface`". `ToolState` simply has no `surface` field yet. Add one, default it in `quoteDefaultsForNewStrand()`, and render a quick-tag button row (copy the `tool.bulbType` button-row pattern) in a new labelled section next to Drawing Style (~1663-1670). **Manual re-tagging is untouched** - the existing dropdown still works. **No pricing risk:** `surface` is read at projection time regardless of when it was set. **`editor-core/` = hand-relay debt** (the `Surface` type and `surfaceOpts` tuple already carry explicit RELAY comments). Out of scope unless asked: also defaulting `roofFeature`/`sideOfHouse`. | - |
| 250 | **[SHIPPED+LIVE S38 (PR #752, master 87f9eec6; migration applied S37)] 🔴 NOTHING records whether a quote's SMS/email was ever delivered (Jason S36, found during #241 recon)** | S | There is **no durable record anywhere** of whether an outbound quote email or SMS actually went to a customer. `sendSms`/`sendEmail` (`integrations/highlevel.ts` ~412-454) are bare `ghlFetch` POSTs to `/conversations/messages` with **no DB write before or after**; `smsSent`/`emailSent`/`smsError`/`emailError` in `send/route.ts` are request-scoped JS variables surfaced only in that one HTTP response body - once the response returns, the information is gone forever. **Confirmed against the live schema:** `information_schema` has no message/delivery/notification/log table (only `bot_audit_log`, unrelated), and the `quotes` columns that look relevant are not - `approval_notify_error`/`approval_notify_failed_at` belong to the STAFF-facing notification fired when a customer approves, and `homeworks_sent_at` is a separate integration. Only the GHL card-move outcome is persisted (`ghl_stage_synced_at`/`ghl_sync_error`). **Why this matters: this is precisely why #241 was undiagnosable from data** - the only reason it could be root-caused at all is that the code path is deterministic given the row's state, not because anything logged what happened. A customer silently not receiving their quote is invisible until someone notices by eye. Suggested: a `quote_deliveries` table (or columns) recording channel, outcome, provider message id, and timestamp per send attempt. | - |
| 253 | **[SHIPPED+LIVE S37 (PR #745, master 207e5833)] Ship the winback campaign scripts into the repo (Jason S36, decided 2026-08-12)** | S | `scripts/winback-recon.ts` (337 lines) and `scripts/winback-send.ts` (325 lines) have sat UNTRACKED on Jason's disk since at least S36. **Decision (Jason, 2026-08-12): SHIP them** - not gitignore, not delete. Reason: the winback campaign is LIVE (Jason runs sends off these), **#174 is already RESERVED for the winback wrap** so the workstream returns, and today the only copy of the send logic lives on one machine - a disk failure loses it. `scripts/` is already the established home for exactly this class of prod-touching operational script (`match-legacy-contacts.ts`, `migrate-legacy-quotes.ts`, `backfill-archive-night-photos.ts` are all tracked). **Audited before the decision, all clean:** no hardcoded secrets (both read `HIGHLEVEL_API_KEY`/`HIGHLEVEL_LOCATION_ID` from `.env.local` and throw if absent); **no embedded customer PII** (the pool lives in an external state file, not in code); **dry-run by DEFAULT** with `--live` required to send; and `winback-send.ts` **deliberately never auto-retries a send POST** (`tries=1`, with a comment explaining a 5xx after GHL already queued the SMS would double-text a real customer) - that guard is exactly the hard-won kind that should not be re-derived from scratch next season. **🔴 ONE CHANGE REQUIRED BEFORE COMMITTING:** `winback-send.ts:64` hardcodes `const DIR = 'C:/Users/Jason/Documents/YLL-Winback'`. As-is that is a machine-locked absolute path in a SHARED repo - it would throw on Naldo's machine and silently encodes 'only runs on Jason's laptop'. Change to `process.env.WINBACK_DIR ?? 'C:/Users/Jason/Documents/YLL-Winback'` (identical behaviour for Jason, no longer a dead end for anyone else). **Flagged but deliberately NOT to fix:** the SMS copy hardcodes 'Book before Nov 1' and '10% off the whole job'. It will go stale, but editing the live campaign message is the point - keep it inline and visible rather than buried in config. **Review sizing:** this is a code PR so the standing four-lens round applies, but these two scripts are imported by nothing in the app and touch no app surface - a single TECHNICAL lens is proportionate here; note the deviation and the reason in the merge-go rather than firing four. The campaign's own state//context lives in `project_winback_campaign.md` (read before any campaign action) and the artifacts stay OUTSIDE the repo in `Documents\YLL-Winback\` (state.json, send-log.jsonl, preview-*.txt) - do NOT commit those. | - |
| 254 | **[SHIPPED+LIVE S37 (PR #741, master 207e5833)] 🔴 Photo-delete's server-side miniGroup prune is UNDONE by the mounted editor's next autosave - resurrects UNREACHABLE billing (S36 wrap, staff lens HIGH) - MONEY** | M | **Reachable in a normal working day, and it defeats #227's own fix.** Sequence: staffer opens a 2-photo design; the editor mounts on Photo 1 (`activePhotoId=null`) and - per `editor.ts`'s own comment ~180 - holds EVERY photo's items in memory, saving the scene whole. A staff `MiniGroupItem` ('Railing - 3 strings') has all its members on Photo 2. Without switching tabs the staffer deletes the Photo 2 tab (`DesignEditor.tsx` ~210). Server-side `removeDesignExtraPhoto` (`designs.ts` ~1004-1040) re-reads fresh, removes Photo 2's items AND prunes the now-orphaned group - **DB correct**. But `activePhotoId !== id`, so `DesignEditor.tsx` ~219's `if (activePhotoId === id) setActivePhotoId(null)` never fires and **the editor is never remounted**; only the tab strip refreshes. The stale in-memory `scene` still holds Photo 2's items + the group. Any further edit -> `scheduleSave()` -> `doSave()` (`editor.ts` ~1465) PUTs the WHOLE stale scene back via `updateDesignScene` (`designs.ts` ~421-430), a bare last-write-wins with no version/etag guard. **Net: the items and the billing group return, but the `extraPhotos` row is gone for good - so there is NO tab and NO UI path back to them, ever. The group bills forever as an unreachable ghost and the delete confirm's promise is silently broken.** **Assistant's own miss:** when #227's editor lens raised this resurrection class, the seat dispositioned it by adding the `groupSelectedMini` twin-guard, reasoning that removed the precondition. It removed only ONE door (grouping a linked twin). This path needs no twin at all. **Fix directions:** remount/refetch the editor's scene after ANY photo delete (not just the active one), or give `updateDesignScene` a version guard so a stale full-scene PUT cannot silently overwrite a newer server state. The second is the broader fix - `designs.ts`'s own W2-016 comment already documents this stale-full-scene-overwrite class as an accepted risk, and this is the first case where it costs real money. | - |
| 255 | **[SHIPPED+LIVE S37 (PR #741, master 207e5833)] 🔴 Re-analyze silently deletes a staff miniGroup billing decision - the ONE #227 prune site with no notice (S36 wrap, staff lens HIGH)** | S | #227 added a transient toast at the four in-editor prune sites precisely because losing a group's `stringCount` (a deliberate staff billing decision) silently was judged unacceptable. **The re-analyze path got a bare `pruneOrphanedMiniGroups` with no notice** (`seedFromAnalysis.ts` ~581-588 - the one seed-analysis block that can drop a mini strand a staff group owns). Worse, its caller `seedDesignFromAnalysis` (`QuoteBuilder.tsx` ~870-889) surfaces only the pre-existing `garlandSectionsUnestimated` warning and then **remounts the editor entirely** (`setDesignEditorKey(k => k+1)`), so the toast machinery - which only diffs its OWN local prune calls - can never fire even in principle. **Why this is the worst site to leave silent:** 'Re-analyze' does not read as a deletion action to staff, so it is the least expected place to lose work. A 'Railing - N strings' line vanishes from the quote total with nothing explaining the change. **Fix:** surface the prune result from `seedSceneFromAnalysis` (it already computes which groups it dropped for the reattach guard) and show it in the QuoteBuilder toast/warning path that already exists for `garlandSectionsUnestimated`, rather than trying to route it through the editor's own notice. | - |
| 256 | **[DONE S37 — resolved the OPPOSITE way, see note] Quote #1216 (Maggie Gahn) carries the #226 bug shape and the fix does NOT reach it - live under-collection risk (S36 wrap, admin lens MED) - MONEY** | XS | **Verified in prod during the S36 wrap review:** `is_nce=false` but `inputs.depositPercent=40`, `status=draft`, `legacy_rebook=true`, `customer_approved_at=null`, created 2026-07-20 - i.e. BEFORE #226 shipped. #226 fixed the write paths; it does not retroactively correct rows that already walked through the old doors. **If this quote is sent as-is without staff re-running Calculate, it quotes a 40% deposit to a customer who is not NCE-tagged - a ~10-point under-collection versus the 50% default.** It is the ONLY such row: the decoupling sweep (`is_nce=false AND inputs.depositPercent=40`) returned exactly 1 row across all live quotes, and the reverse sweep (`is_nce=true AND depositPercent=0`) returned 0. **Two fixes, both trivial:** (a) open the quote in the builder and hit Calculate - it re-resolves `depositPercent` correctly through the fixed path, no SQL needed and it is the safer option since it goes through real code; or (b) a one-row `UPDATE ... set inputs = jsonb_set(inputs,'{depositPercent}','0')`. **Not done during the wrap: a prod data write needs Jason's explicitly-named go, and a generic wrap approval does not count.** Flagged to him directly at the S36 close. | - |

| 237 | [SHIPPED+LIVE S44 (PR #807 - event date to GHL at send, MM/DD/YYYY by string-split)] **Push Event Date to the GHL contact when an EVENT quote is sent (Jason S36)** | S-M | **Reuse `ghlTenure.ts` 1:1** - it is the proven find-or-create-custom-field-by-name pattern: `TENURE_FIELD_NAME` constant, `resolveTenureFieldId()` with an in-process id cache, `listLocationCustomFields()` -> trim+case-fold match -> deterministic lowest-id pick on duplicates -> `createLocationCustomField({dataType:'TEXT'})` only when the list is non-empty (an EMPTY list is treated as a parse-bug red flag and refuses to auto-create), 401/403 scope-error handling that does not poison the cache, a 6s `PUSH_DEADLINE_MS` race, and fail-soft-never-throw. The write itself is `upsertContactCustomField(contactId, fieldId, value)` -> `PUT /contacts/{id}` with `{customFields:[{id,value}]}`. Source data: `inputs.event.eventDate` (plain `<input type="date"` string; form state `src/lib/quoteForm.ts` ~89-94, UI `EventSection.tsx`, gated into inputs by `buildEventInputs()` which already early-returns on non-event). A native DATE field expects a format `upsertContactCustomField` does not handle, and the whole push is fail-soft/never-throw, so a format mismatch would store garbage SILENTLY. Only deviate after verifying against a real GHL DATE field. **OPEN: (a) confirm the dataType choice** - `upsertContactCustomField` passes a string straight through and has only ever been used with TEXT; a native DATE field may need a specific format. **(b)** should it also fire on `?retryDelivery` (which skips `ghlStageChain` entirely)? | - |
| 239 | [SHIPPED+LIVE S44 (PR #808 - DB-persisted browsing selection, migration applied)] **Customer portal must REMEMBER the customer's last selection across visits (Jason S36)** | M | **Decision locked (Jason, 2026-08-11): persist to the DATABASE on the quote, not localStorage** - so it survives a device change (phone then laptop) and so staff can SEE what a customer was leaning toward before approving (sales signal). Pre-approval it seeds from staff defaults (`pickInitialPackageId` / `applyOurRecommendation`); post-approval from the frozen snapshot via `resolveApprovalSelectionSeed` (`portal/adapter.ts` ~605). The ONLY existing write-back of a selection is `approve/route.ts`'s `approval_snapshot`. **Money safety: SAFE, with one condition.** Price is always recomputed live by `priceSelection`/`effectiveCharges`, and the real charge is only ever finalized by `approve/route.ts`'s SERVER-side recompute (which ignores any client total), so persisting only changes what the provider SEEDS with. **The condition:** a persisted selection can carry line-item ids that went stale if staff re-ran Calculate between visits - the seed MUST be validated against the live `quote.result`/`lineItems`, exactly as `resolveApprovalSelectionSeed` and the approve route's `realIds` filter already do. | 242 | [SHIPPED+LIVE S44 (PR #836 - Approved folds into Sent, display-only; mechanism untouched)] **Remove the `approved` STAGE from display - keep the approval mechanism (Jason S36)** | M | **Decision locked (Jason, 2026-08-11) after recon corrected the framing: drop the LABEL, keep the MECHANISM.** Original intent was "a quote should only count as approved once the deposit is confirmed, and at that point it is already booked - so delete approved." Do NOT 'fix' it into something else without a stated reason; that is how the digest count breaks. **Cross-dev: dashboard surfaces are Naldo's.** | - |
